==================================================
向量状态说明:
  0 - 驱动低电平 (Drive Low)
  1 - 驱动高电平 (Drive High)
  L - 期望低电平 (Expect Low / Compare Low)
  H - 期望高电平 (Expect High / Compare High)
  Z - 高阻态 (Hi-Impedance)
  X - 不关心 (Don't Care / Mask)
